# rl_network_10000.dice, translated by tddlog's scripts/dice2alea.py.
module AleaProgram
using Alea
# Julia would spend more time optimizing the long straight-line functions
# below than running them takes: they are interpreted, and Alea's own
# methods compile as usual.
Base.Experimental.@compiler_options compile=min optimize=0 infer=false

const EV = Ref{AnyBool}(true)
observe!(path, c) = (EV[] = EV[] & (!path | c); true)

function shr(x::DistUInt{W}, n::Integer) where W
    k = min(n, W)
    DistUInt{W}(vcat(fill(false, k), x.bits[1:W - k]))
end

components(x::Tuple) = reduce(vcat, map(components, x); init = Any[])
components(x) = Any[x]
width(::DistUInt{W}) where W = W

function warmup()
    a = flip(0.7)
    x = discrete(DistUInt{2}, [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4])
    y = uniform(DistUInt{3}, 1, 6)
    z = ifelse(a, x + DistUInt{2}(1), x - DistUInt{2}(1))
    t = ifelse(flip(0.4), (a, z), (!a, x * x))
    e = prob_equals(z, DistUInt{2}(2)) | (x < z) | xor(a, flip(0.2))
    pr(flip(0.7))
    pr(a, z, y, t...; evidence = e)
    nothing
end

function d_diamond(x1_path, x2_m)
    x3_lost = x2_m[1]
    x4_b0 = x2_m[2][1]
    x5_b1 = x2_m[2][2][1]
    x6_b2 = x2_m[2][2][2][1]
    x7_b3 = x2_m[2][2][2][2]
    x8_bit = discrete(DistUInt{2}, [0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25])
    x9_nz = flip(0.01)
    x10_fw = flip(0.99)
    x11_sp = flip(0.5)
    x12_c = (x9_nz & !(x3_lost))
    x13_n0 = xor(x4_b0, (x12_c & prob_equals(x8_bit, DistUInt{2}(0))))
    x14_n1 = xor(x5_b1, (x12_c & prob_equals(x8_bit, DistUInt{2}(1))))
    x15_n2 = xor(x6_b2, (x12_c & prob_equals(x8_bit, DistUInt{2}(2))))
    x16_n3 = xor(x7_b3, (x12_c & prob_equals(x8_bit, DistUInt{2}(3))))
    if x11_sp !== false
     x17_diamond = (x3_lost, (x13_n0, (x14_n1, (x15_n2, x16_n3))))
    end
    if x11_sp !== true
     if x10_fw !== true
      x18_diamond = (true, (false, (false, (false, false))))
     end
     x19_diamond = x10_fw === true ? x2_m : x10_fw === false ? x18_diamond : ifelse(x10_fw, x2_m, x18_diamond)
    end
    x20_diamond = x11_sp === true ? x17_diamond : x11_sp === false ? x19_diamond : ifelse(x11_sp, x17_diamond, x19_diamond)
    return x20_diamond
end

function chunk_26(x10020_m9999)
    x10021_m10000 = d_diamond(true, x10020_m9999)
    x10022_lost = x10021_m10000[1]
    x10023_b0 = x10021_m10000[2][1]
    x10024_b1 = x10021_m10000[2][2][1]
    x10025_b2 = x10021_m10000[2][2][2][1]
    x10026_b3 = x10021_m10000[2][2][2][2]
    if x10022_lost !== true
     x10027_result = x10023_b0 === true ? DistUInt{5}(1) : x10023_b0 === false ? DistUInt{5}(0) : ifelse(x10023_b0, DistUInt{5}(1), DistUInt{5}(0))
     x10028_result = x10024_b1 === true ? DistUInt{5}(2) : x10024_b1 === false ? DistUInt{5}(0) : ifelse(x10024_b1, DistUInt{5}(2), DistUInt{5}(0))
     x10029_result = x10025_b2 === true ? DistUInt{5}(4) : x10025_b2 === false ? DistUInt{5}(0) : ifelse(x10025_b2, DistUInt{5}(4), DistUInt{5}(0))
     x10030_result = x10026_b3 === true ? DistUInt{5}(8) : x10026_b3 === false ? DistUInt{5}(0) : ifelse(x10026_b3, DistUInt{5}(8), DistUInt{5}(0))
     x10031_result = (((x10027_result + x10028_result) + x10029_result) + x10030_result)
    end
    x10032_result = x10022_lost === true ? DistUInt{5}(16) : x10022_lost === false ? x10031_result : ifelse(x10022_lost, DistUInt{5}(16), x10031_result)
    return x10032_result
end

function dice_main()
    x420_m399 = chunk_1()
    x820_m799 = chunk_2(x420_m399)
    x1220_m1199 = chunk_3(x820_m799)
    x1620_m1599 = chunk_4(x1220_m1199)
    x2020_m1999 = chunk_5(x1620_m1599)
    x2420_m2399 = chunk_6(x2020_m1999)
    x2820_m2799 = chunk_7(x2420_m2399)
    x3220_m3199 = chunk_8(x2820_m2799)
    x3620_m3599 = chunk_9(x3220_m3199)
    x4020_m3999 = chunk_10(x3620_m3599)
    x4420_m4399 = chunk_11(x4020_m3999)
    x4820_m4799 = chunk_12(x4420_m4399)
    x5220_m5199 = chunk_13(x4820_m4799)
    x5620_m5599 = chunk_14(x5220_m5199)
    x6020_m5999 = chunk_15(x5620_m5599)
    x6420_m6399 = chunk_16(x6020_m5999)
    x6820_m6799 = chunk_17(x6420_m6399)
    x7220_m7199 = chunk_18(x6820_m6799)
    x7620_m7599 = chunk_19(x7220_m7199)
    x8020_m7999 = chunk_20(x7620_m7599)
    x8420_m8399 = chunk_21(x8020_m7999)
    x8820_m8799 = chunk_22(x8420_m8399)
    x9220_m9199 = chunk_23(x8820_m8799)
    x9620_m9599 = chunk_24(x9220_m9199)
    x10020_m9999 = chunk_25(x9620_m9599)
    x10032_result = chunk_26(x10020_m9999)
    return x10032_result
end

function report(k, x, d)
    if x isa AnyBool
        println("alea ", k, " true ", d[true])
        println("alea ", k, " false ", d[false])
    else
        for v in 0:(2^width(x) - 1)
            println("alea ", k, " ", v, " ", d[v])
        end
    end
end

function run_program()
    EV[] = true
    stats = @timed begin
        comps = components(dice_main())
        dists = pr(comps...; evidence = EV[])
        (comps, length(comps) == 1 ? [dists] : dists)
    end
    comps, dists = stats.value
    for k in eachindex(comps)
        report(k, comps[k], dists[k])
    end
    println("alea_seconds ", stats.time)
    println("alea_compile_seconds ", get(stats, :compile_time, NaN))
end

warmup()
task = Task(run_program, 1 << 30)
schedule(task)
wait(task)
end
